{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01YNQMECKufcBiiwRpqY1Dp1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_017MR2kFsv8jDNNkYMGN2pSt","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/ac_opf_solver.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver using CasADi and IPOPT\nSolves the optimal power flow problem based on the mathematical model in math-model.md\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Any\n\n@dataclass\nclass Bus:\n    \"\"\"Bus data structure\"\"\"\n    bus_i: int\n    bus_type: int  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=Slack\n    pd: float  # Real power demand (MW)\n    qd: float  # Reactive power demand (MVAr)\n    gs: float  # Shunt conductance\n    bs: float  # Shunt susceptance\n    vm: float  # Voltage magnitude (pu) - initial\n    va: float  # Voltage angle (degrees) - initial\n    vmax: float\n    vmin: float\n    base_kv: float\n\n@dataclass\nclass Generator:\n    \"\"\"Generator data structure\"\"\"\n    gen_bus: int\n    pg: float  # Real power output (MW)\n    qg: float  # Reactive power output (MVAr)\n    qmax: float\n    qmin: float\n    vg: float  # Voltage setpoint\n    pmax: float\n    pmin: float\n    status: int\n\n@dataclass\nclass Branch:\n    \"\"\"Branch data structure\"\"\"\n    f_bus: int\n    t_bus: int\n    br_r: float  # Resistance (pu)\n    br_x: float  # Reactance (pu)\n    br_b: float  # Line charging susceptance (pu)\n    rate_a: float  # MVA limit\n    tap: float\n    shift: float  # Phase shift (degrees)\n    status: int\n    angmin: float\n    angmax: float\n\nclass ACOPFSolver:\n    \"\"\"AC Optimal Power Flow Solver\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, network_file: str, base_mva: float = 100.0):\n        \"\"\"Initialize solver with network data\"\"\"\n        self.base_mva = base_mva\n        self.load_network(network_file)\n        self.slack_bus = None\n        self.identify_slack_bus()\n\n    def load_network(self, network_file: str):\n        \"\"\"Load network from JSON file\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            data = json.load(f)\n\n        self.base_mva = data.get('baseMVA', 100.0)\n\n        # Parse buses\n        self.buses = {}\n        for bus_data in data['bus']:\n            bus = Bus(\n                bus_i=int(bus_data[0]),\n                bus_type=int(bus_data[1]),\n                pd=bus_data[2],\n                qd=bus_data[3],\n                gs=bus_data[4],\n                bs=bus_data[5],\n                vm=bus_data[7],\n                va=bus_data[8],\n                vmax=bus_data[11],\n                vmin=bus_data[12],\n                base_kv=bus_data[9]\n            )\n            self.buses[bus.bus_i] = bus\n\n        # Parse generators\n        self.generators = []\n        for gen_data in data['gen']:\n            gen = Generator(\n                gen_bus=int(gen_data[0]),\n                pg=gen_data[1],\n                qg=gen_data[2],\n                qmax=gen_data[3],\n                qmin=gen_data[4],\n                vg=gen_data[5],\n                pmax=gen_data[8],\n                pmin=gen_data[9],\n                status=int(gen_data[7])\n            )\n            self.generators.append(gen)\n\n        # Parse branches\n        self.branches = []\n        for branch_data in data['branch']:\n            branch = Branch(\n                f_bus=int(branch_data[0]),\n                t_bus=int(branch_data[1]),\n                br_r=branch_data[2],\n                br_x=branch_data[3],\n                br_b=branch_data[4],\n                rate_a=branch_data[5],\n                tap=branch_data[8],\n                shift=branch_data[9],\n                status=int(branch_data[10]),\n                angmin=branch_data[11],\n                angmax=branch_data[12]\n            )\n            self.branches.append(branch)\n\n        # Parse gencost\n        self.gencost = data.get('gencost', [])\n\n    def identify_slack_bus(self):\n        \"\"\"Find the slack bus (type 3)\"\"\"\n        for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n            if bus.bus_type == 3:\n                self.slack_bus = bus_id\n                break\n\n        if self.slack_bus is None:\n            # Use first PV bus as slack if no type 3 exists\n            for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n                if bus.bus_type == 2:\n                    self.slack_bus = bus_id\n                    break\n\n    def build_admittance_matrix(self) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n        \"\"\"Build bus admittance matrix (Y_bus)\"\"\"\n        n_bus = len(self.buses)\n        bus_ids = sorted(self.buses.keys())\n        bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n        Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n        # Add shunt admittances\n        for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n            i = bus_idx[bus_id]\n            Y[i, i] += complex(bus.gs, bus.bs)\n\n        # Add branch admittances\n        for branch in self.branches:\n            if branch.status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            i = bus_idx[branch.f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[branch.t_bus]\n\n            # Series admittance\n            z = complex(branch.br_r, branch.br_x)\n            y_series = 1.0 / z if abs(z) > 1e-10 else 0\n\n            # Tap and shift\n            tap = branch.tap if branch.tap != 0 else 1.0\n            shift_rad = np.radians(branch.shift)\n            t = tap * np.exp(1j * shift_rad)\n\n            # π-section model\n            y_shunt_i = 1j * branch.br_b / 2\n            y_shunt_j = 1j * branch.br_b / 2\n\n            # Add admittances\n            Y[i, i] += y_series / (t * np.conj(t)) + y_shunt_i\n            Y[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt_j\n            Y[i, j] -= y_series / np.conj(t)\n            Y[j, i] -= y_series / t\n\n        return Y, np.array(bus_ids)\n\n    def solve(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Solve AC-OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n        Y, bus_ids = self.build_admittance_matrix()\n        n_bus = len(bus_ids)\n        bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n        # Identify generator buses\n        gen_by_bus = {}\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n            if gen.gen_bus not in gen_by_bus:\n                gen_by_bus[gen.gen_bus] = []\n            gen_by_bus[gen.gen_bus].append(gen_idx)\n\n        # Variables: Vm (voltage magnitude), Va (voltage angle), Pg, Qg\n        Vm = ca.MX.sym('Vm', n_bus)\n        Va = ca.MX.sym('Va', n_bus)\n        Pg = ca.MX.sym('Pg', len(self.generators))\n        Qg = ca.MX.sym('Qg', len(self.generators))\n\n        # Decision variable vector\n        x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        obj = 0\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Get cost coefficients (polynomial model assumed)\n            if gen_idx < len(self.gencost):\n                cost_data = self.gencost[gen_idx]\n                if len(cost_data) >= 7:  # 2nd order polynomial\n                    c2 = cost_data[4]\n                    c1 = cost_data[5]\n                    c0 = cost_data[6]\n                    obj += c2 * Pg[gen_idx]**2 + c1 * Pg[gen_idx] + c0\n\n        # Constraints\n        constraints = []\n        constraint_names = []\n\n        # 1. Slack bus angle = 0\n        slack_idx = bus_idx[self.slack_bus]\n        constraints.append(Va[slack_idx])\n        constraint_names.append('slack_angle')\n\n        # 2. Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            bus = self.buses[bus_id]\n            constraints.append(Vm[i] - bus.vmin)\n            constraints.append(bus.vmax - Vm[i])\n            constraint_names.extend([f'vmin_{bus_id}', f'vmax_{bus_id}'])\n\n        # 3. Generator power bounds and nodal balance\n        for bus_idx_val, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            bus = self.buses[bus_id]\n\n            # Active and reactive power balance\n            p_balance = -bus.pd  # Negative because loads are negative injection\n            q_balance = -bus.qd\n\n            # Add generator injections\n            if bus_id in gen_by_bus:\n                for gen_idx in gen_by_bus[bus_id]:\n                    p_balance += Pg[gen_idx]\n                    q_balance += Qg[gen_idx]\n\n            # Add shunt admittance injection\n            p_balance -= bus.gs * Vm[bus_idx_val]**2\n            q_balance += bus.bs * Vm[bus_idx_val]**2\n\n            # Network power flow (complex form)\n            # P = sum_j Re(V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j))\n            # Q = sum_j Im(V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j))\n\n            for j, bus_j_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n                y_ij = Y[bus_idx_val, j]\n                if abs(y_ij) < 1e-10:\n                    continue\n\n                # V_i = Vm_i * exp(j*Va_i)\n                # V_j = Vm_j * exp(j*Va_j)\n                # Re(V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j))\n                g_ij = y_ij.real\n                b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n                p_flow = (g_ij * Vm[bus_idx_val] * Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[bus_idx_val] - Va[j]) +\n                         b_ij * Vm[bus_idx_val] * Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[bus_idx_val] - Va[j]))\n                q_flow = (g_ij * Vm[bus_idx_val] * Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[bus_idx_val] - Va[j]) -\n                         b_ij * Vm[bus_idx_val] * Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[bus_idx_val] - Va[j]))\n\n                p_balance -= p_flow\n                q_balance -= q_flow\n\n            constraints.append(p_balance)\n            constraints.append(q_balance)\n            constraint_names.extend([f'p_balance_{bus_id}', f'q_balance_{bus_id}'])\n\n        # 4. Generator reactive power bounds\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n            constraints.append(Qg[gen_idx] - gen.qmin)\n            constraints.append(gen.qmax - Qg[gen_idx])\n            constraint_names.extend([f'qmin_gen_{gen_idx}', f'qmax_gen_{gen_idx}'])\n\n        # 5. Generator active power bounds\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] - gen.pmin)\n            constraints.append(gen.pmax - Pg[gen_idx])\n            constraint_names.extend([f'pmin_gen_{gen_idx}', f'pmax_gen_{gen_idx}'])\n\n        # Create NLP\n        g = ca.vertcat(*constraints) if constraints else ca.MX()\n\n        nlp = {'x': x, 'f': obj, 'g': g}\n\n        # Set solver options\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt': {\n                'print_level': 0,\n                'max_iter': 3000,\n                'tol': 1e-6,\n            },\n            'print_time': 0\n        }\n\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initial guess\n        x0 = np.zeros(x.shape[0])\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            x0[i] = self.buses[bus_id].vm\n            x0[n_bus + i] = np.radians(self.buses[bus_id].va)\n\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            x0[2 * n_bus + gen_idx] = gen.pg\n            x0[2 * n_bus + len(self.generators) + gen_idx] = gen.qg\n\n        # Bounds\n        lbx = np.full(x.shape[0], -np.inf)\n        ubx = np.full(x.shape[0], np.inf)\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            bus = self.buses[bus_id]\n            lbx[i] = bus.vmin\n            ubx[i] = bus.vmax\n\n        # Angle bounds (loose)\n        for i in range(n_bus):\n            lbx[n_bus + i] = -np.pi\n            ubx[n_bus + i] = np.pi\n\n        # Generator bounds\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            lbx[2 * n_bus + gen_idx] = gen.pmin\n            ubx[2 * n_bus + gen_idx] = gen.pmax\n            lbx[2 * n_bus + len(self.generators) + gen_idx] = gen.qmin\n            ubx[2 * n_bus + len(self.generators) + gen_idx] = gen.qmax\n\n        # Constraint bounds (equality and inequality)\n        lbg = np.zeros(len(constraints))\n        ubg = np.zeros(len(constraints))\n\n        # Slack angle = 0\n        lbg[0] = ubg[0] = 0\n\n        # Voltage bounds are inequalities (already handled in box constraints)\n        idx = 1\n        for i in range(n_bus):\n            ubg[idx] = 0  # Vm[i] >= vmin\n            lbg[idx] = -np.inf\n            idx += 1\n            lbg[idx] = 0  # vmax >= Vm[i]\n            ubg[idx] = np.inf\n            idx += 1\n\n        # Power balance (equality constraints)\n        for i in range(n_bus):\n            ubg[idx] = lbg[idx] = 0\n            idx += 1\n            ubg[idx] = lbg[idx] = 0\n            idx += 1\n\n        # Q bounds are inequalities\n        for gen_idx in range(len(self.generators)):\n            ubg[idx] = 0\n            lbg[idx] = -np.inf\n            idx += 1\n            lbg[idx] = 0\n            ubg[idx] = np.inf\n            idx += 1\n\n        # P bounds are inequalities\n        for gen_idx in range(len(self.generators)):\n            ubg[idx] = 0\n            lbg[idx] = -np.inf\n            idx += 1\n            lbg[idx] = 0\n            ubg[idx] = np.inf\n            idx += 1\n\n        # Solve\n        sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n        # Extract solution\n        x_opt = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n\n        result = {\n            'success': solver.stats()['return_status'] == 'Solve_Succeeded',\n            'solver_status': solver.stats()['return_status'],\n            'Vm': x_opt[:n_bus],\n            'Va': x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus],\n            'Pg': x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+len(self.generators)],\n            'Qg': x_opt[2*n_bus+len(self.generators):],\n            'bus_ids': bus_ids,\n            'Y': Y,\n            'bus_idx': bus_idx,\n            'obj_value': float(sol['f'])\n        }\n\n        return result\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(solver: ACOPFSolver, result: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows from solution\"\"\"\n    flows = []\n\n    Vm = result['Vm']\n    Va = result['Va']\n    bus_ids = result['bus_ids']\n    Y = result['Y']\n    bus_idx = result['bus_idx']\n\n    for branch in solver.branches:\n        if branch.status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i = bus_idx[branch.f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[branch.t_bus]\n\n        vm_i = Vm[i]\n        va_i = Va[i]\n        vm_j = Vm[j]\n        va_j = Va[j]\n\n        # Compute flow from i to j\n        y_ij = Y[i, j]\n        g_ij = y_ij.real\n        b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n        # Shunt admittance at i\n        y_shunt_i = complex(0, branch.br_b / 2)\n\n        # Current from i\n        p_ij = (g_ij * vm_i * vm_j * np.cos(va_i - va_j) +\n               b_ij * vm_i * vm_j * np.sin(va_i - va_j))\n        q_ij = (g_ij * vm_i * vm_j * np.sin(va_i - va_j) -\n               b_ij * vm_i * vm_j * np.cos(va_i - va_j))\n\n        # Add shunt\n        q_ij += y_shunt_i.imag * vm_i**2\n\n        # Current to j\n        p_ji = (g_ij * vm_j * vm_i * np.cos(va_j - va_i) +\n               b_ij * vm_j * vm_i * np.sin(va_j - va_i))\n        q_ji = (g_ij * vm_j * vm_i * np.sin(va_j - va_i) -\n               b_ij * vm_j * vm_i * np.cos(va_j - va_i))\n\n        # Add shunt\n        y_shunt_j = complex(0, branch.br_b / 2)\n        q_ji += y_shunt_j.imag * vm_j**2\n\n        s_ij = np.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n        s_ji = np.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n        flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch.f_bus,\n            'to_bus': branch.t_bus,\n            'p_from_MW': p_ij,\n            'q_from_MVAr': q_ij,\n            's_from_MVA': s_ij,\n            'p_to_MW': p_ji,\n            'q_to_MVAr': q_ji,\n            's_to_MVA': s_ji,\n            'limit_MVA': branch.rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': 100.0 * max(s_ij, s_ji) / branch.rate_a if branch.rate_a > 0 else 0\n        })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef generate_report(solver: ACOPFSolver, result: Dict[str, Any], flows: List[Dict]) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report in the required format\"\"\"\n\n    Vm = result['Vm']\n    Va = result['Va']\n    Pg = result['Pg']\n    Qg = result['Qg']\n    bus_ids = result['bus_ids']\n    bus_idx = result['bus_idx']\n\n    # Calculate total load\n    total_load_mw = sum(bus.pd for bus in solver.buses.values())\n    total_load_mvar = sum(bus.qd for bus in solver.buses.values())\n\n    # Calculate total generation\n    total_gen_mw = sum(Pg)\n    total_gen_mvar = sum(Qg)\n\n    # Calculate losses\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Find most loaded branches\n    sorted_flows = sorted(flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = sorted_flows[:5]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    # Power balance mismatch check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n    for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n        # Reconstruct power balance\n        p_inj = -solver.buses[bus_id].pd\n        q_inj = -solver.buses[bus_id].qd\n\n        if bus_id in [g.gen_bus for g in solver.generators]:\n            for gen in solver.generators:\n                if gen.gen_bus == bus_id:\n                    idx = solver.generators.index(gen)\n                    p_inj += Pg[idx]\n                    q_inj += Qg[idx]\n\n        # Add shunt\n        p_inj -= solver.buses[bus_id].gs * Vm[i]**2\n        q_inj += solver.buses[bus_id].bs * Vm[i]**2\n\n        # Subtract network flows\n        for j, bus_j_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            y_ij = result['Y'][i, j]\n            g_ij = y_ij.real\n            b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n            p_flow = (g_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * np.cos(Va[i] - Va[j]) +\n                     b_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * np.sin(Va[i] - Va[j]))\n            q_flow = (g_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * np.sin(Va[i] - Va[j]) -\n                     b_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * np.cos(Va[i] - Va[j]))\n\n            p_inj -= p_flow\n            q_inj -= q_flow\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, abs(p_inj))\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, abs(q_inj))\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n        bus = solver.buses[bus_id]\n        if Vm[i] > bus.vmax:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, Vm[i] - bus.vmax)\n        elif Vm[i] < bus.vmin:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, bus.vmin - Vm[i])\n\n    # Branch overload\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n    for flow in flows:\n        if flow['loading_pct'] > 100:\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, flow['loading_pct'] - 100)\n\n    # Collect generators\n    generators = []\n    for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(solver.generators):\n        if gen.status == 0:\n            continue\n        generators.append({\n            'id': gen_idx + 1,\n            'bus': gen.gen_bus,\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg[gen_idx]),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg[gen_idx]),\n            'pmin_MW': gen.pmin,\n            'pmax_MW': gen.pmax,\n            'qmin_MVAr': gen.qmin,\n            'qmax_MVAr': gen.qmax\n        })\n\n    # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=Slack\n    pd: float  # Real power demand (MW)\n    qd: float  # Reactive power demand (MVAr)\n    gs: float  # Shunt conductance\n    bs: float  # Shunt susceptance\n    vm: float  # Voltage magnitude (pu) - initial\n    va: float  # Voltage angle (degrees) - initial\n    vmax: float\n    vmin: float\n    base_kv: float\n\n@dataclass\nclass Generator:\n    \"\"\"Generator data structure\"\"\"\n    gen_bus: int\n    pg: float  # Real power output (MW)\n    qg: float  # Reactive power output (MVAr)\n    qmax: float\n    qmin: float\n    vg: float  # Voltage setpoint\n    pmax: float\n    pmin: float\n    status: int\n\n@dataclass\nclass Branch:\n    \"\"\"Branch data structure\"\"\"\n    f_bus: int\n    t_bus: int\n    br_r: float  # Resistance (pu)\n    br_x: float  # Reactance (pu)\n    br_b: float  # Line charging susceptance (pu)\n    rate_a: float  # MVA limit\n    tap: float\n    shift: float  # Phase shift (degrees)\n    status: int\n    angmin: float\n    angmax: float\n\nclass ACOPFSolver:\n    \"\"\"AC Optimal Power Flow Solver\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, network_file: str, base_mva: float = 100.0):\n        \"\"\"Initialize solver with network data\"\"\"\n        self.base_mva = base_mva\n        self.load_network(network_file)\n        self.slack_bus = None\n        self.identify_slack_bus()\n\n    def load_network(self, network_file: str):\n        \"\"\"Load network from JSON file\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            data = json.load(f)\n\n        self.base_mva = data.get('baseMVA', 100.0)\n\n        # Parse buses\n        self.buses = {}\n        for bus_data in data['bus']:\n            bus = Bus(\n                bus_i=int(bus_data[0]),\n                bus_type=int(bus_data[1]),\n                pd=bus_data[2],\n                qd=bus_data[3],\n                gs=bus_data[4],\n                bs=bus_data[5],\n                vm=bus_data[7],\n                va=bus_data[8],\n                vmax=bus_data[11],\n                vmin=bus_data[12],\n                base_kv=bus_data[9]\n            )\n            self.buses[bus.bus_i] = bus\n\n        # Parse generators\n        self.generators = []\n        for gen_data in data['gen']:\n            gen = Generator(\n                gen_bus=int(gen_data[0]),\n                pg=gen_data[1],\n                qg=gen_data[2],\n                qmax=gen_data[3],\n                qmin=gen_data[4],\n                vg=gen_data[5],\n                pmax=gen_data[8],\n                pmin=gen_data[9],\n                status=int(gen_data[7])\n            )\n            self.generators.append(gen)\n\n        # Parse branches\n        self.branches = []\n        for branch_data in data['branch']:\n            branch = Branch(\n                f_bus=int(branch_data[0]),\n                t_bus=int(branch_data[1]),\n                br_r=branch_data[2],\n                br_x=branch_data[3],\n                br_b=branch_data[4],\n                rate_a=branch_data[5],\n                tap=branch_data[8],\n                shift=branch_data[9],\n                status=int(branch_data[10]),\n                angmin=branch_data[11],\n                angmax=branch_data[12]\n            )\n            self.branches.append(branch)\n\n        # Parse gencost\n        self.gencost = data.get('gencost', [])\n\n    def identify_slack_bus(self):\n        \"\"\"Find the slack bus (type 3)\"\"\"\n        for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n            if bus.bus_type == 3:\n                self.slack_bus = bus_id\n                break\n\n        if self.slack_bus is None:\n            # Use first PV bus as slack if no type 3 exists\n            for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n                if bus.bus_type == 2:\n                    self.slack_bus = bus_id\n                    break\n\n    def build_admittance_matrix(self) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n        \"\"\"Build bus admittance matrix (Y_bus)\"\"\"\n        n_bus = len(self.buses)\n        bus_ids = sorted(self.buses.keys())\n        bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n        Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n        # Add shunt admittances\n        for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n            i = bus_idx[bus_id]\n            Y[i, i] += complex(bus.gs, bus.bs)\n\n        # Add branch admittances\n        for branch in self.branches:\n            if branch.status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            i = bus_idx[branch.f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[branch.t_bus]\n\n            # Series admittance\n            z = complex(branch.br_r, branch.br_x)\n            y_series = 1.0 / z if abs(z) > 1e-10 else 0\n\n            # Tap and shift\n            tap = branch.tap if branch.tap != 0 else 1.0\n            shift_rad = np.radians(branch.shift)\n            t = tap * np.exp(1j * shift_rad)\n\n            # π-section model\n            y_shunt_i = 1j * branch.br_b / 2\n            y_shunt_j = 1j * branch.br_b / 2\n\n            # Add admittances\n            Y[i, i] += y_series / (t * np.conj(t)) + y_shunt_i\n            Y[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt_j\n            Y[i, j] -= y_series / np.conj(t)\n            Y[j, i] -= y_series / t\n\n        return Y, np.array(bus_ids)\n\n    def solve(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Solve AC-OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n        Y, bus_ids = self.build_admittance_matrix()\n        n_bus = len(bus_ids)\n        bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n        # Identify generator buses\n        gen_by_bus = {}\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n            if gen.gen_bus not in gen_by_bus:\n                gen_by_bus[gen.gen_bus] = []\n            gen_by_bus[gen.gen_bus].append(gen_idx)\n\n        # Variables: Vm (voltage magnitude), Va (voltage angle), Pg, Qg\n        Vm = ca.MX.sym('Vm', n_bus)\n        Va = ca.MX.sym('Va', n_bus)\n        Pg = ca.MX.sym('Pg', len(self.generators))\n        Qg = ca.MX.sym('Qg', len(self.generators))\n\n        # Decision variable vector\n        x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        obj = 0\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Get cost coefficients (polynomial model assumed)\n            if gen_idx < len(self.gencost):\n                cost_data = self.gencost[gen_idx]\n                if len(cost_data) >= 7:  # 2nd order polynomial\n                    c2 = cost_data[4]\n                    c1 = cost_data[5]\n                    c0 = cost_data[6]\n                    obj += c2 * Pg[gen_idx]**2 + c1 * Pg[gen_idx] + c0\n\n        # Constraints\n        constraints = []\n        constraint_names = []\n\n        # 1. Slack bus angle = 0\n        slack_idx = bus_idx[self.slack_bus]\n        constraints.append(Va[slack_idx])\n        constraint_names.append('slack_angle')\n\n        # 2. Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            bus = self.buses[bus_id]\n            constraints.append(Vm[i] - bus.vmin)\n            constraints.append(bus.vmax - Vm[i])\n            constraint_names.extend([f'vmin_{bus_id}', f'vmax_{bus_id}'])\n\n        # 3. Generator power bounds and nodal balance\n        for bus_idx_val, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            bus = self.buses[bus_id]\n\n            # Active and reactive power balance\n            p_balance = -bus.pd  # Negative because loads are negative injection\n            q_balance = -bus.qd\n\n            # Add generator injections\n            if bus_id in gen_by_bus:\n                for gen_idx in gen_by_bus[bus_id]:\n                    p_balance += Pg[gen_idx]\n                    q_balance += Qg[gen_idx]\n\n            # Add shunt admittance injection\n            p_balance -= bus.gs * Vm[bus_idx_val]**2\n            q_balance += bus.bs * Vm[bus_idx_val]**2\n\n            # Network power flow (complex form)\n            # P = sum_j Re(V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j))\n            # Q = sum_j Im(V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j))\n\n            for j, bus_j_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n                y_ij = Y[bus_idx_val, j]\n                if abs(y_ij) < 1e-10:\n                    continue\n\n                # V_i = Vm_i * exp(j*Va_i)\n                # V_j = Vm_j * exp(j*Va_j)\n                # Re(V_i * conj(Y_ij * V_j))\n                g_ij = y_ij.real\n                b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n                p_flow = (g_ij * Vm[bus_idx_val] * Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[bus_idx_val] - Va[j]) +\n                         b_ij * Vm[bus_idx_val] * Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[bus_idx_val] - Va[j]))\n                q_flow = (g_ij * Vm[bus_idx_val] * Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[bus_idx_val] - Va[j]) -\n                         b_ij * Vm[bus_idx_val] * Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[bus_idx_val] - Va[j]))\n\n                p_balance -= p_flow\n                q_balance -= q_flow\n\n            constraints.append(p_balance)\n            constraints.append(q_balance)\n            constraint_names.extend([f'p_balance_{bus_id}', f'q_balance_{bus_id}'])\n\n        # 4. Generator reactive power bounds\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n            constraints.append(Qg[gen_idx] - gen.qmin)\n            constraints.append(gen.qmax - Qg[gen_idx])\n            constraint_names.extend([f'qmin_gen_{gen_idx}', f'qmax_gen_{gen_idx}'])\n\n        # 5. Generator active power bounds\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n            constraints.append(Pg[gen_idx] - gen.pmin)\n            constraints.append(gen.pmax - Pg[gen_idx])\n            constraint_names.extend([f'pmin_gen_{gen_idx}', f'pmax_gen_{gen_idx}'])\n\n        # Create NLP\n        g = ca.vertcat(*constraints) if constraints else ca.MX()\n\n        nlp = {'x': x, 'f': obj, 'g': g}\n\n        # Set solver options\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt': {\n                'print_level': 0,\n                'max_iter': 3000,\n                'tol': 1e-6,\n            },\n            'print_time': 0\n        }\n\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initial guess\n        x0 = np.zeros(x.shape[0])\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            x0[i] = self.buses[bus_id].vm\n            x0[n_bus + i] = np.radians(self.buses[bus_id].va)\n\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            x0[2 * n_bus + gen_idx] = gen.pg\n            x0[2 * n_bus + len(self.generators) + gen_idx] = gen.qg\n\n        # Bounds\n        lbx = np.full(x.shape[0], -np.inf)\n        ubx = np.full(x.shape[0], np.inf)\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            bus = self.buses[bus_id]\n            lbx[i] = bus.vmin\n            ubx[i] = bus.vmax\n\n        # Angle bounds (loose)\n        for i in range(n_bus):\n            lbx[n_bus + i] = -np.pi\n            ubx[n_bus + i] = np.pi\n\n        # Generator bounds\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            lbx[2 * n_bus + gen_idx] = gen.pmin\n            ubx[2 * n_bus + gen_idx] = gen.pmax\n            lbx[2 * n_bus + len(self.generators) + gen_idx] = gen.qmin\n            ubx[2 * n_bus + len(self.generators) + gen_idx] = gen.qmax\n\n        # Constraint bounds (equality and inequality)\n        lbg = np.zeros(len(constraints))\n        ubg = np.zeros(len(constraints))\n\n        # Slack angle = 0\n        lbg[0] = ubg[0] = 0\n\n        # Voltage bounds are inequalities (already handled in box constraints)\n        idx = 1\n        for i in range(n_bus):\n            ubg[idx] = 0  # Vm[i] >= vmin\n            lbg[idx] = -np.inf\n            idx += 1\n            lbg[idx] = 0  # vmax >= Vm[i]\n            ubg[idx] = np.inf\n            idx += 1\n\n        # Power balance (equality constraints)\n        for i in range(n_bus):\n            ubg[idx] = lbg[idx] = 0\n            idx += 1\n            ubg[idx] = lbg[idx] = 0\n            idx += 1\n\n        # Q bounds are inequalities\n        for gen_idx in range(len(self.generators)):\n            ubg[idx] = 0\n            lbg[idx] = -np.inf\n            idx += 1\n            lbg[idx] = 0\n            ubg[idx] = np.inf\n            idx += 1\n\n        # P bounds are inequalities\n        for gen_idx in range(len(self.generators)):\n            ubg[idx] = 0\n            lbg[idx] = -np.inf\n            idx += 1\n            lbg[idx] = 0\n            ubg[idx] = np.inf\n            idx += 1\n\n        # Solve\n        sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n        # Extract solution\n        x_opt = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n\n        result = {\n            'success': solver.stats()['return_status'] == 'Solve_Succeeded',\n            'solver_status': solver.stats()['return_status'],\n            'Vm': x_opt[:n_bus],\n            'Va': x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus],\n            'Pg': x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+len(self.generators)],\n            'Qg': x_opt[2*n_bus+len(self.generators):],\n            'bus_ids': bus_ids,\n            'Y': Y,\n            'bus_idx': bus_idx,\n            'obj_value': float(sol['f'])\n        }\n\n        return result\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(solver: ACOPFSolver, result: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows from solution\"\"\"\n    flows = []\n\n    Vm = result['Vm']\n    Va = result['Va']\n    bus_ids = result['bus_ids']\n    Y = result['Y']\n    bus_idx = result['bus_idx']\n\n    for branch in solver.branches:\n        if branch.status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i = bus_idx[branch.f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[branch.t_bus]\n\n        vm_i = Vm[i]\n        va_i = Va[i]\n        vm_j = Vm[j]\n        va_j = Va[j]\n\n        # Compute flow from i to j\n        y_ij = Y[i, j]\n        g_ij = y_ij.real\n        b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n        # Shunt admittance at i\n        y_shunt_i = complex(0, branch.br_b / 2)\n\n        # Current from i\n        p_ij = (g_ij * vm_i * vm_j * np.cos(va_i - va_j) +\n               b_ij * vm_i * vm_j * np.sin(va_i - va_j))\n        q_ij = (g_ij * vm_i * vm_j * np.sin(va_i - va_j) -\n               b_ij * vm_i * vm_j * np.cos(va_i - va_j))\n\n        # Add shunt\n        q_ij += y_shunt_i.imag * vm_i**2\n\n        # Current to j\n        p_ji = (g_ij * vm_j * vm_i * np.cos(va_j - va_i) +\n               b_ij * vm_j * vm_i * np.sin(va_j - va_i))\n        q_ji = (g_ij * vm_j * vm_i * np.sin(va_j - va_i) -\n               b_ij * vm_j * vm_i * np.cos(va_j - va_i))\n\n        # Add shunt\n        y_shunt_j = complex(0, branch.br_b / 2)\n        q_ji += y_shunt_j.imag * vm_j**2\n\n        s_ij = np.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n        s_ji = np.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n        flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch.f_bus,\n            'to_bus': branch.t_bus,\n            'p_from_MW': p_ij,\n            'q_from_MVAr': q_ij,\n            's_from_MVA': s_ij,\n            'p_to_MW': p_ji,\n            'q_to_MVAr': q_ji,\n            's_to_MVA': s_ji,\n            'limit_MVA': branch.rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': 100.0 * max(s_ij, s_ji) / branch.rate_a if branch.rate_a > 0 else 0\n        })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef generate_report(solver: ACOPFSolver, result: Dict[str, Any], flows: List[Dict]) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report in the required format\"\"\"\n\n    Vm = result['Vm']\n    Va = result['Va']\n    Pg = result['Pg']\n    Qg = result['Qg']\n    bus_ids = result['bus_ids']\n    bus_idx = result['bus_idx']\n\n    # Calculate total load\n    total_load_mw = sum(bus.pd for bus in solver.buses.values())\n    total_load_mvar = sum(bus.qd for bus in solver.buses.values())\n\n    # Calculate total generation\n    total_gen_mw = sum(Pg)\n    total_gen_mvar = sum(Qg)\n\n    # Calculate losses\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Find most loaded branches\n    sorted_flows = sorted(flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = sorted_flows[:5]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    # Power balance mismatch check\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n    for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n        # Reconstruct power balance\n        p_inj = -solver.buses[bus_id].pd\n        q_inj = -solver.buses[bus_id].qd\n\n        if bus_id in [g.gen_bus for g in solver.generators]:\n            for gen in solver.generators:\n                if gen.gen_bus == bus_id:\n                    idx = solver.generators.index(gen)\n                    p_inj += Pg[idx]\n                    q_inj += Qg[idx]\n\n        # Add shunt\n        p_inj -= solver.buses[bus_id].gs * Vm[i]**2\n        q_inj += solver.buses[bus_id].bs * Vm[i]**2\n\n        # Subtract network flows\n        for j, bus_j_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            y_ij = result['Y'][i, j]\n            g_ij = y_ij.real\n            b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n            p_flow = (g_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * np.cos(Va[i] - Va[j]) +\n                     b_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * np.sin(Va[i] - Va[j]))\n            q_flow = (g_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * np.sin(Va[i] - Va[j]) -\n                     b_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * np.cos(Va[i] - Va[j]))\n\n            p_inj -= p_flow\n            q_inj -= q_flow\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, abs(p_inj))\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, abs(q_inj))\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n        bus = solver.buses[bus_id]\n        if Vm[i] > bus.vmax:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, Vm[i] - bus.vmax)\n        elif Vm[i] < bus.vmin:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, bus.vmin - Vm[i])\n\n    # Branch overload\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n    for flow in flows:\n        if flow['loading_pct'] > 100:\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, flow['loading_pct'] - 100)\n\n    # Collect generators\n    generators = []\n    for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(solver.generators):\n        if gen.status == 0:\n            continue\n        generators.append({\n            'id': gen_idx + 1,\n            'bus': gen.gen_bus,\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg[gen_idx]),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg[gen_idx]),\n            'pmin_MW': gen.pmin,\n            'pmax_MW': gen.pmax,\n            'qmin_MVAr': gen.qmin,\n            'qmax_MVAr': gen.qmax\n        })\n\n    # Collect buses\n    buses_report = []\n    for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n        bus = solver.buses[bus_id]\n        buses_report.append({\n            'id': bus_id,\n            'vm_pu': float(Vm[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(np.degrees(Va[i])),\n            'vmin_pu': bus.vmin,\n            'vmax_pu': bus.vmax\n        })\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(result['obj_value']),\n            'total_load_MW': total_load_mw,\n            'total_load_MVAr': total_load_mvar,\n            'total_generation_MW': total_gen_mw,\n            'total_generation_MVAr': total_gen_mvar,\n            'total_losses_MW': total_losses_mw,\n            'solver_status': result['solver_status']\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses_report,\n        'most_loaded_branches': [\n            {\n                'from_bus': int(f['from_bus']),\n                'to_bus': int(f['to_bus']),\n                'loading_pct': float(f['loading_pct']),\n                'flow_from_MVA': float(f['s_from_MVA']),\n                'flow_to_MVA': float(f['s_to_MVA']),\n                'limit_MVA': float(f['limit_MVA'])\n            }\n            for f in most_loaded\n        ],\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': float(max_p_mismatch),\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': float(max_q_mismatch),\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_voltage_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    # Solve AC-OPF\n    solver = ACOPFSolver('/root/network.json')\n    print(\"Solving AC-OPF...\")\n    result = solver.solve()\n\n    print(f\"Solver status: {result['solver_status']}\")\n    print(f\"Objective value: {result['obj_value']:.2f}\")\n\n    # Compute branch flows\n    flows = compute_branch_flows(solver, result)\n\n    # Generate report\n    report = generate_report(solver, result, flows)\n\n    # Save report\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"Report saved to /root/report.json\")\n    print(f\"Total generation: {report['summary']['total_generation_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total load: {report['summary']['total_load_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Losses: {report['summary']['total_losses_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

branch.status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            i = bus_idx[branch.f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[branch.t_bus]\n\n            # Series admittance\n            z = complex(branch.br_r,           # Add admittances\n            Y[i, i] += y_series / (t * np.conj(t)) + y_shunt_i\n            Y[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt_j\n            Y[i, j] -= y_series / np.conj(t)\n            Y[j, 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=Slack\n    pd: float  # Real power demand (MW)\n    qd: float  # Reactive power demand (MVAr)\n    gs: float  # Shunt conductance\n    bs: float  # Shunt susceptance\n    vm: float  # Voltage magnitude (pu) - initial\n    va: float  # Voltage angle (degrees) - initial\n    vmax: float\n    vmin: float\n    base_kv: float\n\n@dataclass\nclass Generator:\n    \"\"\"Generator data structure\"\"\"\n    gen_bus: int\n    pg: float  # Real power output (MW)\n    qg: float  # Reactive power output (MVAr)\n    qmax: float\n    qmin: float\n    vg: float  # Voltage setpoint\n    pmax: float\n    pmin: float\n    status: int\n\n@dataclass\nclass Branch:\n    \"\"\"Branch data structure\"\"\"\n    f_bus: int\n    t_bus: int\n    br_r: float  # Resistance (pu)\n    br_x: float  # Reactance (pu)\n    br_b: float  # Line charging susceptance (pu)\n    rate_a: float  # MVA limit\n    tap: float\n    shift: float  # Phase shift (degrees)\n    status: int\n    angmin: float\n    angmax: float\n\nclass ACOPFSolver:\n    \"\"\"AC Optimal Power Flow Solver\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, network_file: str, base_mva: float = 100.0):\n        \"\"\"Initialize solver with network data\"\"\"\n        self.base_mva = base_mva\n        self.load_network(network_file)\n        self.slack_bus = None\n        self.identify_slack_bus()\n\n    def load_network(self, network_file: str):\n        \"\"\"Load network from JSON file\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            data = json.load(f)\n\n        self.base_mva = data.get('baseMVA', 100.0)\n\n        # Parse buses\n        self.buses = {}\n        for bus_data in data['bus']:\n            bus = Bus(\n                bus_i=int(bus_data[0]),\n                bus_type=int(bus_data[1]),\n                pd=bus_data[2],\n                qd=bus_data[3],\n                gs=bus_data[4],\n                bs=bus_data[5],\n                vm=bus_data[7],\n                va=bus_data[8],\n                vmax=bus_data[11],\n                vmin=bus_data[12],\n                base_kv=bus_data[9]\n            )\n            self.buses[bus.bus_i] = bus\n\n        # Parse gencost\n        self.gencost = data.get('gencost', [])\n\n    def identify_slack_bus(self):\n        \"\"\"Find the slack bus (type 3)\"\"\"\n        for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n            if bus.bus_type == 3:\n                self.slack_bus = bus_id\n                break\n\n        if self.slack_bus is None:\n            # Use first PV bus as slack if no type 3 exists\n            for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n                if bus.bus_type == 2:\n                    self.slack_bus = bus_id\n                    break\n\n    def build_admittance_matrix(self) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n        \"\"\"Build bus admittance matrix (Y_bus)\"\"\"\n        n_bus = len(self.buses)\n        bus_ids = sorted(self.buses.keys())\n        bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n        Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n        # Add shunt admittances\n        for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n            i = bus_idx[bus_id]\n            Y[i, i] += complex(bus.gs, bus.bs)\n\n        # Add branch admittances\n        for branch in self.branches:\n            if branch.status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            i = bus_idx[branch.f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[branch.t_bus]\n\n            # Series admittance\n            z = complex(branch.br_r, branch.br_x)\n            y_series = 1.0 / z if abs(z) > 1e-10 else 0\n\n            # Tap and shift\n            tap = branch.tap if branch.tap != 0 else 1.0\n            shift_rad = np.radians(branch.shift)\n            t = tap * np.exp(1j * shift_rad)\n\n            # π-section model\n            y_shunt_i = 1j * branch.br_b / 2\n            y_shunt_j = 1j * branch.br_b / 2\n\n            # Add admittances\n            Y[i, i] += y_series / (t * np.conj(t)) + y_shunt_i\n            Y[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt_j\n            Y[i, j] -= y_series / np.conj(t)\n            Y[j, i] -= y_series / t\n\n        return Y, np.array(bus_ids)\n\n    def solve(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Solve AC-OPF problem using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n        Y, bus_ids = self.build_admittance_matrix()\n        n_bus = len(bus_ids)\n        bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n        n_gen = len(self.generators)\n\n        # Identify generator buses\n        gen_by_bus = {}\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n            if gen.gen_bus not in gen_by_bus:\n                gen_by_bus[gen.gen_bus] = []\n            gen_by_bus[gen.gen_bus].append(gen_idx)\n\n        # Decision variables: Vm (voltage magnitude), Va (voltage angle), Pg, Qg\n        Vm = ca.MX.sym('Vm', n_bus)\n        Va = ca.MX.sym('Va', n_bus)\n        Pg = ca.MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n        Qg = ca.MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        obj = 0\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Get cost coefficients\n            if gen_idx < len(self.gencost):\n                cost_data = self.gencost[gen_idx]\n                if len(cost_data) >= 7:  # 2nd order polynomial\n                    c2 = cost_data[4]\n                    c1 = cost_data[5]\n                    c0 = cost_data[6]\n                    obj += c2 * Pg[gen_idx]**2 + c1 * Pg[gen_idx] + c0\n\n        # Build constraints\n        constraints = []\n\n        # 1. Slack bus voltage angle = 0\n        slack_idx = bus_idx[self.slack_bus]\n        constraints.append(Va[slack_idx])\n\n        # 2. Power balance equations (P and Q at each bus)\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            bus = self.buses[bus_id]\n\n            # Real power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_network\n            p_gen = 0\n            q_gen = 0\n            if bus_id in gen_by_bus:\n                for gen_idx in gen_by_bus[bus_id]:\n                    p_gen = p_gen + Pg[gen_idx]\n                    q_gen = q_gen + Qg[gen_idx]\n\n            # Compute network injections\n            p_net = 0\n            q_net = 0\n            for j, bus_j_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n                y_ij = Y[i, j]\n                g_ij = y_ij.real\n                b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n                p_flow = (g_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[i] - Va[j]) +\n                         b_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[i] - Va[j]))\n                q_flow = (g_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[i] - Va[j]) -\n                         b_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[i] - Va[j]))\n\n                p_net = p_net + p_flow\n                q_net = q_net + q_flow\n\n            # Power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_network\n            p_balance = p_gen - bus.pd - bus.gs * Vm[i]**2 - p_net\n            q_balance = q_gen - bus.qd + bus.bs * Vm[i]**2 - q_net\n\n            constraints.append(p_balance)\n            constraints.append(q_balance)\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(*constraints) if constraints else ca.MX()\n\n        # Setup NLP\n        nlp = {'x': x, 'f': obj, 'g': g}\n\n        # Solver options\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt': {\n                'print_level': 0,\n                'max_iter': 3000,\n                'tol': 1e-4,\n                'acceptable_tol': 1e-3,\n                'acceptable_iter': 10,\n            },\n            'print_time': 0\n        }\n\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initial guess\n        x0 = np.zeros(x.shape[0])\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            x0[i] = self.buses[bus_id].vm\n            x0[n_bus + i] = np.radians(self.buses[bus_id].va)\n\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            x0[2 * n_bus + gen_idx] = gen.pg\n            x0[2 * n_bus + n_gen + gen_idx] = gen.qg\n\n        # Variable bounds\n        lbx = np.full(x.shape[0], -np.inf)\n        ubx = np.full(x.shape[0], np.inf)\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            bus = self.buses[bus_id]\n            lbx[i] = bus.vmin\n            ubx[i] = bus.vmax\n\n        # Angle bounds\n        for i in range(n_bus):\n            lbx[n_bus + i] = -np.pi\n            ubx[n_bus + i] = np.pi\n\n        # Generator bounds\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            lbx[2 * n_bus + gen_idx] = gen.pmin\n            ubx[2 * n_bus + gen_idx] = gen.pmax\n            lbx[2 * n_bus + n_gen + gen_idx] = gen.qmin\n            ubx[2 * n_bus + n_gen + gen_idx] = gen.qmax\n\n        # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=Slack\n    pd: float  # Real power demand (MW)\n    qd: float  # Reactive power demand (MVAr)\n    gs: float  # Shunt conductance\n    bs: float  # Shunt susceptance\n    vm: float  # Voltage magnitude (pu) - initial\n    va: float  # Voltage angle (degrees) - initial\n    vmax: float\n    vmin: float\n    base_kv: float\n\n@dataclass\nclass Generator:\n    \"\"\"Generator data structure\"\"\"\n    gen_bus: int\n    pg: float  # Real power output (MW)\n    qg: float  # Reactive power output (MVAr)\n    qmax: float\n    qmin: float\n    vg: float  # Voltage setpoint\n    pmax: float\n    pmin: float\n    status: int\n\n@dataclass\nclass Branch:\n    \"\"\"Branch data structure\"\"\"\n    f_bus: int\n    t_bus: int\n    br_r: float  # Resistance (pu)\n    br_x: float  # Reactance (pu)\n    br_b: float  # Line charging susceptance (pu)\n    rate_a: float  # MVA limit\n    tap: float\n    shift: float  # Phase shift (degrees)\n    status: int\n    angmin: float\n    angmax: float\n\nclass ACOPFSolver:\n    \"\"\"AC Optimal Power Flow Solver\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, network_file: str, base_mva: float = 100.0):\n        \"\"\"Initialize solver with network data\"\"\"\n        self.base_mva = base_mva\n        self.load_network(network_file)\n        self.slack_bus = None\n        self.identify_slack_bus()\n\n    def load_network(self, network_file: str):\n        \"\"\"Load network from JSON file\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            data = json.load(f)\n\n        self.base_mva = data.get('baseMVA', 100.0)\n\n        # Parse buses\n        self.buses = {}\n        for bus_data in data['bus']:\n            bus = Bus(\n                bus_i=int(bus_data[0]),\n                bus_type=int(bus_data[1]),\n                pd=bus_data[2],\n                qd=bus_data[3],\n                gs=bus_data[4],\n                bs=bus_data[5],\n                vm=bus_data[7],\n                va=bus_data[8],\n                vmax=bus_data[11],\n                vmin=bus_data[12],\n                base_kv=bus_data[9]\n            )\n            self.buses[bus.bus_i] = bus\n\n        # Parse generators\n        self.generators = []\n        for gen_data in data['gen']:\n            gen = Generator(\n                gen_bus=int(gen_data[0]),\n                pg=gen_data[1],\n                qg=gen_data[2],\n                qmax=gen_data[3],\n                qmin=gen_data[4],\n                vg=gen_data[5],\n                pmax=gen_data[8],\n                pmin=gen_data[9],\n                status=int(gen_data[7])\n            )\n            self.generators.append(gen)\n\n        # Parse branches\n        self.branches = []\n        for branch_data in data['branch']:\n            branch = Branch(\n                f_bus=int(branch_data[0]),\n                t_bus=int(branch_data[1]),\n                br_r=branch_data[2],\n                br_x=branch_data[3],\n                br_b=branch_data[4],\n                rate_a=branch_data[5],\n                tap=branch_data[8],\n                shift=branch_data[9],\n                status=int(branch_data[10]),\n                angmin=branch_data[11],\n                angmax=branch_data[12]\n            )\n            self.branches.append(branch)\n\n        # Parse gencost\n        self.gencost = data.get('gencost', [])\n\n    def identify_slack_bus(self):\n        \"\"\"Find the slack bus (type 3)\"\"\"\n        for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n            if bus.bus_type == 3:\n                self.slack_bus = bus_id\n                break\n\n        if self.slack_bus is None:\n            # Use first PV bus as slack if no type 3 exists\n            for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n                if bus.bus_type == 2:\n                    self.slack_bus = bus_id\n                    break\n\n    def build_admittance_matrix(self) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n        \"\"\"Build bus admittance matrix (Y_bus)\"\"\"\n        n_bus = len(self.buses)\n        bus_ids = sorted(self.buses.keys())\n        bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n        Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n        # Add shunt admittances\n        for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n            i = bus_idx[bus_id]\n            Y[i, i] += complex(bus.gs, bus.bs)\n\n        # Add branch admittances\n        for branch in self.branches:\n            if branch.status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            i = bus_idx[branch.f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[branch.t_bus]\n\n            # Series admittance\n            z = complex(branch.br_r, branch.br_x)\n            y_series = 1.0 / z if abs(z) > 1e-10 else 0\n\n            # Tap and shift\n            tap = branch.tap if branch.tap != 0 else 1.0\n            shift_rad = np.radians(branch.shift)\n            t = tap * np.exp(1j * shift_rad)\n\n            # π-section model\n            y_shunt_i = 1j * branch.br_b / 2\n            y_shunt_j = 1j * branch.br_b / 2\n\n            # Add admittances\n            Y[i, i] += y_series / (t * np.conj(t)) + y_shunt_i\n            Y[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt_j\n            Y[i, j] -= y_series / np.conj(t)\n            Y[j, i] -= y_series / t\n\n        return Y, np.array(bus_ids)\n\n    def solve(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Solve AC-OPF problem using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n        Y, bus_ids = self.build_admittance_matrix()\n        n_bus = len(bus_ids)\n        bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n        n_gen = len(self.generators)\n\n        # Identify generator buses\n        gen_by_bus = {}\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n            if gen.gen_bus not in gen_by_bus:\n                gen_by_bus[gen.gen_bus] = []\n            gen_by_bus[gen.gen_bus].append(gen_idx)\n\n        # Decision variables: Vm (voltage magnitude), Va (voltage angle), Pg, Qg\n        Vm = ca.MX.sym('Vm', n_bus)\n        Va = ca.MX.sym('Va', n_bus)\n        Pg = ca.MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n        Qg = ca.MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        obj = 0\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Get cost coefficients\n            if gen_idx < len(self.gencost):\n                cost_data = self.gencost[gen_idx]\n                if len(cost_data) >= 7:  # 2nd order polynomial\n                    c2 = cost_data[4]\n                    c1 = cost_data[5]\n                    c0 = cost_data[6]\n                    obj += c2 * Pg[gen_idx]**2 + c1 * Pg[gen_idx] + c0\n\n        # Build constraints\n        constraints = []\n\n        # 1. Slack bus voltage angle = 0\n        slack_idx = bus_idx[self.slack_bus]\n        constraints.append(Va[slack_idx])\n\n        # 2. Power balance equations (P and Q at each bus)\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            bus = self.buses[bus_id]\n\n            # Real power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_network\n            p_gen = 0\n            q_gen = 0\n            if bus_id in gen_by_bus:\n                for gen_idx in gen_by_bus[bus_id]:\n                    p_gen = p_gen + Pg[gen_idx]\n                    q_gen = q_gen + Qg[gen_idx]\n\n            # Compute network injections\n            p_net = 0\n            q_net = 0\n            for j, bus_j_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n                y_ij = Y[i, j]\n                g_ij = y_ij.real\n                b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n                p_flow = (g_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[i] - Va[j]) +\n                         b_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[i] - Va[j]))\n                q_flow = (g_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * ca.sin(Va[i] - Va[j]) -\n                         b_ij * Vm[i] * Vm[j] * ca.cos(Va[i] - Va[j]))\n\n                p_net = p_net + p_flow\n                q_net = q_net + q_flow\n\n            # Power balance: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_network\n            p_balance = p_gen - bus.pd - bus.gs * Vm[i]**2 - p_net\n            q_balance = q_gen - bus.qd + bus.bs * Vm[i]**2 - q_net\n\n            constraints.append(p_balance)\n            constraints.append(q_balance)\n\n        g = ca.vertcat(*constraints) if constraints else ca.MX()\n\n        # Setup NLP\n        nlp = {'x': x, 'f': obj, 'g': g}\n\n        # Solver options\n        opts = {\n            'ipopt': {\n                'print_level': 0,\n                'max_iter': 3000,\n                'tol': 1e-4,\n                'acceptable_tol': 1e-3,\n                'acceptable_iter': 10,\n            },\n            'print_time': 0\n        }\n\n        solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n        # Initial guess\n        x0 = np.zeros(x.shape[0])\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            x0[i] = self.buses[bus_id].vm\n            x0[n_bus + i] = np.radians(self.buses[bus_id].va)\n\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            x0[2 * n_bus + gen_idx] = gen.pg\n            x0[2 * n_bus + n_gen + gen_idx] = gen.qg\n\n        # Variable bounds\n        lbx = np.full(x.shape[0], -np.inf)\n        ubx = np.full(x.shape[0], np.inf)\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            bus = self.buses[bus_id]\n            lbx[i] = bus.vmin\n            ubx[i] = bus.vmax\n\n        # Angle bounds\n        for i in range(n_bus):\n            lbx[n_bus + i] = -np.pi\n            ubx[n_bus + i] = np.pi\n\n        # Generator bounds\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            lbx[2 * n_bus + gen_idx] = gen.pmin\n            ubx[2 * n_bus + gen_idx] = gen.pmax\n            lbx[2 * n_bus + n_gen + gen_idx] = gen.qmin\n            ubx[2 * n_bus + n_gen + gen_idx] = gen.qmax\n\n        # Constraint bounds\n        lbg = np.zeros(len(constraints))\n        ubg = np.zeros(len(constraints))\n\n        # Solve\n        sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n        # Extract solution\n        x_opt = np.array(sol['x']).flatten()\n\n        result = {\n            'success': solver.stats()['return_status'] == 'Solve_Succeeded',\n            'solver_status': solver.stats()['return_status'],\n            'Vm': x_opt[:n_bus],\n            'Va': x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus],\n            'Pg': x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen],\n            'Qg': x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:],\n            'bus_ids': bus_ids,\n            'Y': Y,\n            'bus_idx': bus_idx,\n            'obj_value': float(sol['f'])\n        }\n\n        return result\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(solver: ACOPFSolver, result: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows from solution\"\"\"\n    flows = []\n\n    Vm = result['Vm']\n    Va = result['Va']\n    bus_ids = result['bus_ids']\n    Y = result['Y']\n    bus_idx = result['bus_idx']\n\n    for branch in solver.branches:\n        if branch.status == 0:\n            continue\n\n        i = bus_idx[branch.f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[branch.t_bus]\n\n        vm_i = Vm[i]\n        va_i = Va[i]\n        vm_j = Vm[j]\n        va_j = Va[j]\n\n        # Compute flow from i to j\n        y_ij = Y[i, j]\n        g_ij = y_ij.real\n        b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n        p_ij = (g_ij * vm_i * vm_j * np.cos(va_i - va_j) +\n               b_ij * vm_i * vm_j * np.sin(va_i - va_j))\n        q_ij = (g_ij * vm_i * vm_j * np.sin(va_i - va_j) -\n               b_ij * vm_i * vm_j * np.cos(va_i - va_j))\n\n        # Add shunt at i\n        q_ij += (branch.br_b / 2) * vm_i**2\n\n        # Compute flow from j to i\n        p_ji = (g_ij * vm_j * vm_i * np.cos(va_j - va_i) +\n               b_ij * vm_j * vm_i * np.sin(va_j - va_i))\n        q_ji = (g_ij * vm_j * vm_i * np.sin(va_j - va_i) -\n               b_ij * vm_j * vm_i * np.cos(va_j - va_i))\n\n        # Add shunt at j\n        q_ji += (branch.br_b / 2) * vm_j**2\n\n        s_ij = np.sqrt(p_ij**2 + q_ij**2)\n        s_ji = np.sqrt(p_ji**2 + q_ji**2)\n\n        flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch.f_bus,\n            'to_bus': branch.t_bus,\n            'p_from_MW': p_ij,\n            'q_from_MVAr': q_ij,\n            's_from_MVA': s_ij,\n            'p_to_MW': p_ji,\n            'q_to_MVAr': q_ji,\n            's_to_MVA': s_ji,\n            'limit_MVA': branch.rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': 100.0 * max(s_ij, s_ji) / branch.rate_a if branch.rate_a > 0 else 0\n        })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef generate_report(solver: ACOPFSolver, result: Dict[str, Any], flows: List[Dict]) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report in the required format\"\"\"\n\n    Vm = result['Vm']\n    Va = result['Va']\n    Pg = result['Pg']\n    Qg = result['Qg']\n    bus_ids = result['bus_ids']\n\n    def to_python(val):\n        \"\"\"Convert numpy types to Python native types\"\"\"\n        if isinstance(val, (np.integer, np.floating)):\n            return float(val)\n        return val\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    total_load_mw = sum(bus.pd for bus in solver.buses.values())\n    total_load_mvar = sum(bus.qd for bus in solver.buses.values())\n    total_gen_mw = sum(Pg)\n    total_gen_mvar = sum(Qg)\n    total_losses_mw = total_gen_mw - total_load_mw\n\n    # Most loaded branches\n    sorted_flows = sorted(flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = sorted_flows[:5]\n\n    # Feasibility metrics\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n    for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n        bus = solver.buses[bus_id]\n        p_balance = -bus.pd - bus.gs * Vm[i]**2\n        q_balance = -bus.qd + bus.bs * Vm[i]**2\n\n        # Add generator contributions\n        for j, gen in enumerate(solver.generators):\n            if gen.gen_bus == bus_id:\n                p_balance += Pg[j]\n                q_balance += Qg[j]\n\n        # Subtract network flows\n        for k, bus_k_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n            y_ik = result['Y'][i, k]\n            g_ik = y_ik.real\n            b_ik = y_ik.imag\n\n            p_flow = (g_ik * Vm[i] * Vm[k] * np.cos(Va[i] - Va[k]) +\n                     b_ik * Vm[i] * Vm[k] * np.sin(Va[i] - Va[k]))\n            q_flow = (g_ik * Vm[i] * Vm[k] * np.sin(Va[i] - Va[k]) -\n                     b_ik * Vm[i] * Vm[k] * np.cos(Va[i] - Va[k]))\n\n            p_balance -= p_flow\n            q_balance -= q_flow\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, abs(p_balance))\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, abs(q_balance))\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n        bus = solver.buses[bus_id]\n        if Vm[i] > bus.vmax:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, Vm[i] - bus.vmax)\n        elif Vm[i] < bus.vmin:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, bus.vmin - Vm[i])\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n    for flow in flows:\n        if flow['loading_pct'] > 100:\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, flow['loading_pct'] - 100)\n\n    # Build generators list\n    generators = []\n    for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(solver.generators):\n        if gen.status == 0:\n            continue\n        generators.append({\n            'id': int(gen_idx + 1),\n            'bus': int(gen.gen_bus),\n            'pg_MW': to_python(Pg[gen_idx]),\n            'qg_MVAr': to_python(Qg[gen_idx]),\n            'pmin_MW': to_python(gen.pmin),\n            'pmax_MW': to_python(gen.pmax),\n            'qmin_MVAr': to_python(gen.qmin),\n            'qmax_MVAr': to_python(gen.qmax)\n        })\n\n    # Build buses list\n    buses_report = []\n    for i, bus_id in enumerate(bus_ids):\n        bus = solver.buses[bus_id]\n        buses_report.append({\n            'id': int(bus_id),\n            'vm_pu': to_python(Vm[i]),\n            'va_deg': to_python(np.degrees(Va[i])),\n            'vmin_pu': to_python(bus.vmin),\n            'vmax_pu': to_python(bus.vmax)\n        })\n\n    # Construct report\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': to_python(result['obj_value']),\n            'total_load_MW': to_python(total_load_mw),\n            'total_load_MVAr': to_python(total_load_mvar),\n            'total_generation_MW': to_python(total_gen_mw),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': to_python(total_gen_mvar),\n            'total_losses_MW': to_python(total_losses_mw),\n            'solver_status': result['solver_status']\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses_report,\n        'most_loaded_branches': [\n            {\n                'from_bus': int(f['from_bus']),\n                'to_bus': int(f['to_bus']),\n                'loading_pct': to_python(f['loading_pct']),\n                'flow_from_MVA': to_python(f['s_from_MVA']),\n                'flow_to_MVA': to_python(f['s_to_MVA']),\n                'limit_MVA': to_python(f['limit_MVA'])\n            }\n            for f in most_loaded\n        ],\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': to_python(max_p_mismatch),\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': to_python(max_q_mismatch),\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': to_python(max_voltage_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': to_python(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=Slack\n    pd: float  # Real power demand (MW)\n    qd: float  # Reactive power demand (MVAr)\n    gs: float  # Shunt conductance\n    bs: float  # Shunt susceptance\n    vm: float  # Voltage magnitude (pu) - initial\n    va: float  # Reactive power output (MVAr)\n    qmax: float\n    qmin: float\n    vg: float  # Voltage setpoint\n    pmax: float\n    pmin: float\n    status: int\n\n@dataclass\nclass Branch:\n    \"\"\"Branch data structure\"\"\"\n    f_bus: int\n    t_bus: int\n    br_r: float  # Resistance (pu)\n    br_x: float  # Reactance (pu)\n    br_b: float  # Line charging susceptance (pu)\n    rate_a: float  # MVA limit\n    tap: float\n    shift: float  # Phase shift (degrees)\n    status: int\n    angmin: float\n    angmax: float\n\nclass ACOPFSolver:\n    \"\"\"AC Optimal Power Flow Solver\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, network_file: str, base_mva: float = 100.0):\n        \"\"\"Initialize solver with network data\"\"\"\n        self.base_mva = base_mva\n        self.load_network(network_file)\n        self.slack_bus = None\n        self.identify_slack_bus()\n\n    def load_network(self, network_file: str):\n        \"\"\"Load network from JSON file\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            data = json.load(f)\n\n        self.base_mva = data.get('baseMVA', 100.0)\n\n        # Parse buses\n        self.buses = {}\n        for bus_data in data['bus']:\n            bus = Bus(\n                bus_i=int(bus_data[0]),\n                bus_type=int(bus_data[1]),\n                pd=bus_data[2],\n                qd=bus_data[3],\n                gs=bus_data[4],\n                bs=bus_data[5],\n                vm=bus_data[7],\n                va=bus_data[8],\n                vmax=bus_data[11],\n                vmin=bus_data[12],\n                base_kv=bus_data[9]\n            )\n            self.buses[bus.bus_i] = bus\n\n        # Use first PV bus as slack if no type 3 exists\n            for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n                if bus.bus_type == 2:\n                    self.slack_bus = bus_id\n                    break\n\n    def build_admittance_matrix(self) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n        \"\"\"Build bus admittance matrix (Y_bus)\"\"\"\n        n_bus = len(self.buses)\n        bus_ids = sorted(self.buses.keys())\n        bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n        Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n        # Add shunt admittances\n        for bus_id, bus in self.buses.items():\n            i = bus_idx[bus_id]\n            Y[i, i] += complex(bus.gs, bus.bs)\n\n        # Add branch admittances\n        for branch in self.branches:\n            if branch.status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            i = bus_idx[branch.f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[branch.t_bus]\n\n            # Series admittance\n            z = complex(branch.br_r, branch.br_x)\n            y_series = 1.0 / z if abs(z) > 1e-10 else 0\n\n            # Tap and shift\n            tap = branch.tap if branch.tap != 0 else 1.0\n            shift_rad = np.radians(branch.shift)\n            t = tap * np.exp(1j * shift_rad)\n\n            # π-section model\n            y_shunt_i = 1j * branch.br_b / 2\n            y_shunt_j = 1j * branch.br_b / 2\n\n            # Add admittances\n            Y[i, i] += y_series / (t * np.conj(t)) + y_shunt_i\n            Y[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt_j\n            Y[i, j] -= y_series / np.conj(t)\n            Y[j, i] -= y_series / t\n\n        return Y, np.array(bus_ids)\n\n    def solve(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Solve AC-OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n        Y, bus_ids = self.build_admittance_matrix()\n        n_bus = len(bus_ids)\n        bus_idx = {bid: i for i, bid in enumerate(bus_ids)}\n\n        # Identify generator buses\n        gen_by_bus = {}\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n            if gen.gen_bus not in gen_by_bus:\n                gen_by_bus[gen.gen_bus] = []\n            gen_by_bus[gen.gen_bus].append(gen_idx)\n\n        # Variables: Vm (voltage magnitude), Va (voltage angle), Pg, Qg\n        Vm = ca.MX.sym('Vm', n_bus)\n        Va = ca.MX.sym('Va', n_bus)\n        Pg = ca.MX.sym('Pg', len(self.generators))\n        Qg = ca.MX.sym('Qg', len(self.generators))\n\n        # Decision variable vector\n        x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        obj = 0\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(self.generators):\n            if gen.status == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Get cost coefficients (polynomial model assumed)\n            if gen_idx < len(self.gencost):\n                cost_data = self.gencost[gen_idx]\n                if len(cost_data) >= 7:  # 2nd order polynomial\n                    c2 = cost_data[4]\n                    c1 = cost_data[5]\n                    c0 = cost_data[6]\n                    obj += c2 * Pg[gen_idx]**2 + c1 * Pg[gen_idx] + c0\n\n        #
